## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人伙伴。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试，或者帮你盯着 PR 的 CI，过了告诉你。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos, and I'll tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 예를 들어 어떤 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트를 찾아 고쳐 달라고 하거나, 메일이나 일정을 확인해 달라고 하면 바로 시작할 수 있어요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性を保証する通信、UDPは保証しない代わりに速くて軽い通信です。

TCPはデータが届いたか確認し、欠けたものを再送し、順番も揃えて渡します。UDPはそうした確認をせず、送りっぱなしにします。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para actualizar o limpiar tu rama local antes de compartirla, siempre que nadie más tenga esos commits. Usa merge para integrar una rama ya publicada o compartida, porque reescribir su historial rompería el trabajo de los demás.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是面向连接、可靠的传输：先握手建立连接，保证数据不丢、不乱、按序到达；UDP 是无连接、不保证可靠的传输，发得快、开销小，但丢包和乱序要自己处理。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is yours alone and not pushed yet, so you can tidy it and keep history linear. Merge when others already have the branch or you want the record of when the branches joined.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用 `bun test` 后面直接跟文件路径就行：

```bash
bun test tests/foo.test.ts
```

也可以只写文件名的一部分，Bun 会匹配路径里包含它的测试文件：

```bash
bun test foo
```

如果还想只跑文件里的某一个测试，加 `-t`（按测试名匹配）：

```bash
bun test tests/foo.test.ts -t "某个测试名"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to disk before the change is applied to the actual data files. After a crash, the database replays that log to recover committed work and discard anything half-finished, which keeps data durable and consistent without writing every change straight to its final location.
```

}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>

.k-card{background:var(--d-card);border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:12px;overflow:hidden}
.k-list{list-style:none;margin:0;padding:0}
.k-row{display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;padding:11px 12px;border-top:1px solid var(--d-line);font-size:15px;cursor:pointer}
.k-row:first-child{border-top:0}
.k-row-main{color:var(--d-ink)}
.k-row-sub{font-size:12px;color:var(--d-muted);margin-left:8px}
.k-row-value{font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums;color:var(--d-ink)}
.k-row-del{display … ducter.set('expenses', list);
  render();
}

var got = ducter.get('expenses');
if(got && typeof got.then === 'function') got.then(setList); else setList(got);
ducter.onChange(function(){
  var v = ducter.get('expenses');
  if(v && typeof v.then === 'function') v.then(setList); else setList(v);
});

document.getElementById('save').addEventListener('click', function(){
  var day = document.getElementById('day').value.trim();
  var item = document.getElementById('item').value.trim();
  var amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('amt').value);
  if(!day || !item || !(amount > 0)) return;
  list.push({ id: String(Date.now()) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,7), day: day, item: item, amount: Math.round(amount*100)/100 });
  document.getElementById('item').value = '';
  document.getElementById('amt').value = '';
  persist();
});

document.getElementById('days').addEventListener('click', function(ev){
  var del = ev.target.closest('[data-del]');
  if(del){
    var id = del.getAttribute('data-del');
    list = list.filter(function(e){ return e.id !== id; });
    persist();
    return;
  }
  var row = ev.target.closest('.k-row');
  if(row) row.classList.toggle('open');
});
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

### app-prs-known-en

```
(earlier) user: which of my PRs are still waiting on someone?
(earlier) partner: Four: wey-gu/ducter#412 (compaction cut) waits on Weizhen's review since Monday; nowledge-co/mem#10287 (retry on 502) on CI, failing; nowledge-co/hawdb#1592 (flaky compaction test) on Alice since Tuesday; wey-gu/ducter#418 (apps bar) is a draft.
--- step 1: user says: make that a little page I can keep and tick off as they land
  [partner message] Done: a page with your four PRs. Tick each one off as it lands, and it keeps the count.
  [an app card under the message: made "PRs waiting on someone", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1876 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">Landed</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="n">0 / 4</div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Tick each one off as it lands</div></div>
<h2 class="k-h">Waiting on someone</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<style>
.k-row label{display:flex;gap:10px;align-items:flex-start;width:100%;cursor:pointer}
.k-row input[type=checkbox]{margin-top:4px;width:18px;height:18px;accent-color:var(--d-accent);flex:none}
.done .k-row-main{text-decoration:line-through;color:var(--d-muted)}
</style>
<script>
var DEFAULT=ducter.get("default") || [];
var prs=DEFAULT;
function esc(s){return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){return {"&":"&amp;","<":"&lt;",">":"&gt;",'"':"&quot;","'":"&#39;"}[c];});}
function render(){
  var list=document.getElementById("list"),html="",n=0;
  prs.forEach(function(p,i){
    if(p.done)n++;
    html+='<div class="k-row'+(p.done?' done':'')+'"><label><input type="checkbox" data-i="'+i+'"'+(p.done?' checked':'')+'><span class="k-row-main">'+esc(p.repo)+' · '+esc(p.what)+'</span></label><div class="k-row-sub">'+esc(p.wait)+'</div></div>';
  });
  list.innerHTML=html;
  document.getElementById("n").textContent=n+" / "+prs.length;
  document.getElementById("sub").textContent=(prs.length-n)+" still waiting";
  ducter.summary(n+" of "+prs.length+" landed");
}
list.addEventListener;
document.getElementById("list").addEventListener("change",function(e){
  var i=e.target.getAttribute("data-i");
  if(i===null)return;
  prs[+i].done=e.target.checked;
  ducter.set("prs",prs);
  render();
});
Promise.resolve(ducter.get("prs")).then(function(v){
  if(Array.isArray(v)&&v.length)prs=v;
  render();
});
ducter.onChange(function(){
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get("prs")).then(function(v){
    if(Array.isArray(v)&&v.length){prs=v;render();}
  });

  [an app card under the message: changed "出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2571 characters): <main class="k-app">
<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">出差花销合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count"></div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <p class="k-empty" id="empty">还没记</p>
</div>
<details class="k-add">
  <summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-20"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>分类</label><select class="k-select" id="cat"><option>交通</option><option>住宿</option></select></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-input k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>
</details>
</main>
<script>
const $ = id => document.getElementById(id);
function fmt(n){ return "¥" + Number(n).toLocaleString("en-US"); }
function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("items") || [];
  const total = xs.reduce((s, x) => s + (Number(x.amt) || 0), 0);
  if (!xs.length) {
    $("total").className = "k-sum-value k-none";
    $("total").textContent = "还没记";
  … ount").textContent = xs.length + " 笔";
    ducter.summary(xs.length + " 笔,合计 " + fmt(total));
  }
  const list = $("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  xs.forEach(x => {
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    const main = document.createElement("div");
    main.className = "k-row-main";
    main.textContent = x.trip;
    const sub = document.createElement("div");
    sub.className = "k-row-sub";
    sub.textContent = [x.day, x.cat, x.note].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
    const val = document.createElement("div");
    val.className = "k-row-value";
    val.textContent = fmt(x.amt);
    row.append(main, sub, val);
    list.append(row);
  });
  $("empty").hidden = xs.length > 0;
}
$("save").onclick = () => {
  const trip = $("trip").value.trim();
  const amt = Number($("amt").value);
  if (!trip || !amt) return;
  const xs = ducter.get("items") || [];
  xs.push({ trip, day: $("day").value.trim(), cat: $("cat").value, amt });
  ducter.set("items", xs);
  $("amt").value = "";
  $("day").value = "";
  $("trip").value = "";
  $("cat").selectedIndex = 0;
  $("save").closest("details").open = false;
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 07:00 上海虹桥→北京南"},{"trip":"北京出差","day":"2026-11-20","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话：「这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻」

请在 TTL 测试里再补一条测试，覆盖缓存项恰好到期的那一刻（假时钟推进到 TTL 整点时）：
- 到期时刻之前一刻（如 TTL-1ms）：值仍然可取；
- 恰好到期时刻：按现有语义判断（请确认并在测试里写明边界是“已过期”还是“仍有效”），与实现保持一致；
- 到期之后：值不可取。
继续使用假时钟，不要引入真实计时（sleep）。

完成标准：新测试通过，且现有 TTL 测试在多次运行中仍稳定通过；在回报里给出测试结果。
范围外：不要合并分支，不要推送，不要改动其他功能的代码。改动在当前任务分支上提交即可。
```
